									Section.Objects["Content"] = Menu.Objects:Scroll(Section.Objects["Frame"], true, UDim.new(0, 0), UDim.new(0, 11), UDim.new(0, 11))
									Section.Objects["Line"] = Menu.Objects:Frame(nil, "Light Background", nil, nil, nil, 0, Section.Objects["Frame"], UDim2.new(0, 9, 0, -2), UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 2))
									Section.Objects["Text"] = Menu.Objects:Text(true, Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Section.Objects["Frame"], UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0), UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 20), Section.Name, "Light Text")
									Section.Objects["Open"] = Menu.Objects:Holder(nil, Section.Objects["Frame"], UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 1), UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -2), nil)
								end

								function Section:Initialise(Size)
									Section.Objects["Frame"].Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, Size)
									Section.Objects["Line"].Size = UDim2.new(0, (Section.Objects["Text"].TextBounds.X + 6), 0, 2)

									for Content, Value in next, Section.Content do
										if not Content.NoCallback then
											Utils:Call(Content.Set, Content, Content.State, Content.Alpha)
										end
									end
								end

								if Section.Side == "middle" then
									Section.Page.Sections.Middle = Section.Page.Sections.Middle or {}
									Section.Page.Sections.Middle[#Section.Page.Sections.Middle + 1] = Section
								else
									Section.Page.Sections[Section.Side][#Section.Page.Sections[Section.Side] + 1] = Section
								end

								return setmetatable(Section, { __index = Menu.Sections })
							end

							function Menu:SettingsPage()
								local Self = self
								local Settings = Self:Page({ Name = "Settings" })
								local Theming, Extra, Presets = Settings:Section({ Name = "Theming" }), Settings:Section({ Name = "Extra" }), Settings:Section({ Name = "Presets", Side = "Right" })

								for _, Theme in pairs({
									"Accent", "Lighter Background", "Light Background", "Dark Background",
									"Outline", "Lighter Inline", "Light Inline", "Dark Inline", "Light Text", "Dark Text",
								}) do
									Theming:Label({ Name = Theme }):Picker({
										State = Menu.Themes[Theme],
										Flag = Theme,
										Callback = function(State)
											Menu.Presets:Update(Theme, State)
										end,
									})
								end
								return Settings
							end

							function Menu.Sections:Holder(Properties)
								local Properties = (Properties or {})
								local Content = {
									Objects = {},
									Window = self.Window,
									Page = self.Page,
									Section = (self.Section or self),
									Flag = (Utils:Property(Properties, "Flag", "Pointer")),
									Small = (Utils:Property(Properties, "Decreased", "Minified", "Smaller", "Small")),
									Callback = (Utils:Property(Properties, "Callback", "Function", "Func", "Return")),
								}

								Content.Objects["Holder"] = Menu.Objects:Holder(nil, self.Objects["Content"], nil, UDim2.new(1, (Content.Small and -12 or 0), 0, 0), true)
								Menu.Objects:List(nil, Content.Objects["Holder"], nil, "Center")

								function Content:Set(State)
									Content.Objects["Holder"].Visible = State
								end

								Content.Objects["Holder"].AutomaticSize = "Y"
								Content.Objects["Content"] = Content.Objects["Holder"]

								if Content.Flag then Flags[Content.Flag] = Content end
								local ContentObj = setmetatable(Content, { __index = Menu.Sections })
								Content.Callback(ContentObj)
								return ContentObj
							end

							function Menu.Sections:Label(Properties)
								local Properties = (Properties or {})
								local Content = {
									Objects = {},
									Window = self.Window,
									Page = self.Page,
									Section = (self.Section or self),
									Offset = 0,
									Name = (Utils:Property(Properties, "Name", "Text") or "Content"),
									Center = (Utils:Property(Properties, "Center", "Middle") or false),
									Inlay = (Utils:Property(Properties, "Inlayed", "Inlay") or false),
									Wide = (Utils:Property(Properties, "Wide", "Wider") or false),
									Tooltip = (Utils:Property(Properties, "Tooltip", "Tip") or nil),
								}

								if Content.Tooltip then
									Content.Name = Content.Name .. ' <font color="rgb(180, 180, 180)">(?)</font>'
								end

								Content.Objects["Holder"] = Menu.Objects:Holder(nil, self.Objects["Content"], nil, UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, (Content.Inlay and 12 or 16)))
								Content.Objects["Text"] = Menu.Objects:Text(true, nil, Content.Objects["Holder"], UDim2.new(0, (Content.Wide and 25 or 37), 0, (Content.Inlay and 1 or -1)), UDim2.new(1, -37, 1, 0), Content.Name, "Light Text", (Content.Center and "Center"))

								function Content:Picker(Properties) return Menu.Sections.Addons:Picker(Content, Properties) end
								function Content:Bind(Properties) return Menu.Sections.Addons:Bind(Content, Properties) end

								if Content.Inlay then Content.Offset = 2 end
								return Content
							end

							function Menu.Sections:Button(Properties)
								local Properties = (Properties or {})
								local Content = {
									Objects = {},
									Window = self.Window,
									Page = self.Page,
									Section = (self.Section or self),
									Name = (Utils:Property(Properties, "Name", "Text") or "Content"),
									Wide = (Utils:Property(Properties, "Wide", "Wider") or false),
									Callback = (Utils:Property(Properties, "Callback", "Function", "Func", "Return")),
								}

								Content.Objects["Holder"] = Menu.Objects:Holder(nil, self.Objects["Content"], nil, UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 23))
								Content.Objects["Outline"], Content.Objects["Frame"] = Menu.Objects:Outline(nil, Content.Objects["Holder"], UDim2.new(0, (Content.Wide and 24 or 36), 0, 4), UDim2.new(1, (Content.Wide and -49 or -69), 0, 18), "Lighter Background")
								Menu.Objects:Gradient(Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0), -90, 0.7, Content.Objects["Frame"])
								Content.Objects["Text"] = Menu.Objects:Text(true, nil, Content.Objects["Frame"], UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), Content.Name, "Light Text", "Center")
								Content.Objects["Button"] = Menu.Objects:Button(Content.Objects["Holder"])

								Utils:Connect(Content.Objects["Button"].MouseButton1Down, function()
									Utils:Thread(function()
										Content.Objects["Frame"].BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
										task.wait(0.075)
										Content.Objects["Frame"].BackgroundTransparency = 0
									end)
									Utils:Call(Content.Callback)
								end)

								return Content
							end

							function Menu.Sections:Box(Properties)
								local Properties = (Properties or {})
								local Content = {
									Objects = {},
									Window = self.Window,
									Page = self.Page,
									Section = (self.Section or self),
									State = (Utils:Property(Properties, "State", "Current", "Default", "Def") or ""),
									Holder = (Utils:Property(Properties, "Name", "Text", "Holder", "PlaceHolder", "PlaceHolderText") or ""),
									Confirm = (Utils:Property(Properties, "Confirmation", "Confirm") or false),
									Wide = (Utils:Property(Properties, "Wide", "Wider") or false),
									Flag = (Utils:Property(Properties, "Flag", "Pointer") or (self.Page.Name .. "_" .. self.Name .. "_" .. (Properties.Name or Properties.Text or "Option"))),
									Callback = (Utils:Property(Properties, "Callback", "Function", "Func", "Return")),
								}

								Content.Objects["Holder"] = Menu.Objects:Holder(nil, self.Objects["Content"], nil, UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 25))
								Content.Objects["Outline"], Content.Objects["Outline_Frame"] = Menu.Objects:Outline(nil, Content.Objects["Holder"], UDim2.new(0, (Content.Wide and 24 or 36), 0, 4), UDim2.new(1, (Content.Wide and -49 or -69), 0, 20), "Outline")
								Content.Objects["Frame"] = Menu.Objects:Frame(nil, "Lighter Background", nil, nil, nil, 0, Content.Objects["Outline_Frame"], UDim2.new(0, 1, 0, 1), UDim2.new(1, -2, 1, -2))
								Content.Objects["Value"] = Menu.Objects:Box(nil, Content.Objects["Frame"], UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 0), UDim2.new(1, -12, 1, 0), "", Content.Holder, "Light Text", "Left", nil, "AtEnd")
								Content.Objects["Button"] = Menu.Objects:Button(Content.Objects["Holder"])

								function Content:Set(State)
									Content.State = State
									Content.Objects["Value"].Text = Content.State
									Utils:Call(Content.Callback, Content.State)
								end

								function Content:Get(Raw) return Content.State end

								Content.Section.Content[Content] = true
								if Content.Flag then Flags[Content.Flag] = Content end
								return Content
							end

							function Menu.Sections:List(Properties)
								local Properties = (Properties or {})
								local Content = {
									Objects = {},
									Items = {},
									Window = self.Window,
									Page = self.Page,
									Section = (self.Section or self),
									State = (Utils:Property(Properties, "State", "Current", "Default", "Def") or 1),
									Shown = (Utils:Property(Properties, "Shown", "Listed", "Viewed") or 10),
									Maximum = (Utils:Property(Properties, "Maximum", "Max", "Most", "Maximal") or 1),
									Minimum = (Utils:Property(Properties, "Minimum", "Min", "Least", "Minimal") or 1),
									Options = (Utils:Property(Properties, "Options", "List") or { "1", "2", "3", "4", "5" }),
									Selected = (Utils:Property(Properties, "Global", "Selected", "Select")),
									Wide = (Utils:Property(Properties, "Wide", "Wider") or false),
									Flag = (Utils:Property(Properties, "Flag", "Pointer") or (self.Page.Name .. "_" .. self.Name .. "_" .. (Properties.Name or Properties.Text or "Option"))),
									Callback = (Utils:Property(Properties, "Callback", "Function", "Func", "Return")),
									NoCallback = (Utils:Property(Properties, "NoCallback", "DontCallback", "IgnoreCallback", "Ignore") or false),
								}

								Content.Objects["Holder"] = Menu.Objects:Holder(nil, self.Objects["Content"], nil, UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, ((Content.Shown * 18) + 6 + 2)))
								Content.Objects["Outline"], Content.Objects["Frame"] = Menu.Objects:Outline(nil, Content.Objects["Holder"], UDim2.new(0, (Content.Wide and 24 or 36), 0, 2), UDim2.new(1, (Content.Wide and -49 or -69), 0, (Content.Shown * 18) + 6), "Lighter Background")
								Menu.Objects:Gradient(Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0), -90, 0.7, Content.Objects["Frame"])
								Content.Objects["Content"] = Menu.Objects:Scroll(Content.Objects["Frame"])

								function Content:Set(State)
									Content.State = State
									if typeof(Content.State) == "table" then
										for key, item in next, Content.Items do item:Set(Content.State[key] == true) end
									else
										for key, item in next, Content.Items do
											if key == Content.State then item:Set(true) elseif item.State then item:Set(false) end
										end
									end
									Utils:Call(Content.Callback, Content.State)
								end

								function Content:Get(Raw) return Content.State end

								function Content:Insert(Value)
									if not Content.Items[Value] then
										local Item = { Connections = {}, Objects = {}, State = false }
										Item.Objects["Holder"] = Menu.Objects:Holder(nil, Content.Objects["Content"], nil, UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18))
										Item.Objects["Text"] = Menu.Objects:Text(false, nil, Item.Objects["Holder"], UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0), UDim2.new(1, -24, 1, 0), Value, "Light Text")
										Item.Objects["Button"] = Menu.Objects:Button(Item.Objects["Holder"])

										function Item:Remove()
											for _, c in next, Item.Connections do c:Disconnect() end
											for _, o in next, Item.Objects do o:Remove() end
											Item = nil
										end

										function Item:Show(visible) Item.Objects["Holder"].Visible = visible end
										function Item:Set(state)
											Item.State = state
											Menu.Objects:Update(Item.Objects["Text"], { TextColor3 = (Item.State and "Accent" or "Light Text") })
										end

										Item.Connections[#Item.Connections + 1] = Item.Objects["Button"].MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
											Content:Set(Value)
										end)
										Content.Items[Value] = Item
									end
								end

								function Content:Refresh(Options, Selected)
									Content.Options = Options
									Content.Selected = (Selected or Content.Options)
									for key, item in next, Content.Items do
										if not Content.Options[key] then item:Remove() Content.Items[key] = nil end
									end
									for key, _ in next, Content.Options do
										if not Content.Items[key] then Content:Insert(key) end
									end
									for key, _ in next, Content.Options do
										Content.Items[key]:Show(Content.Selected[key])
									end
								end

								Content.Section.Content[Content] = true
								local OptionsTable = {}
								for _, Value in next, Content.Options do OptionsTable[Value] = true end
								Content:Refresh(OptionsTable)
								if Content.Flag then Flags[Content.Flag] = Content end
								return Content
							end

							function Menu.Sections:Toggle(Properties)
								local Properties = (Properties or {})
								local Content = {
									Objects = {},
									Window = self.Window,
									Page = self.Page,
									Section = (self.Section or self),
									Offset = 0,
									Name = (Utils:Property(Properties, "Name", "Text") or "Content"),
									State = (Utils:Property(Properties, "State", "Current", "Default", "Def") or false),
									Flag = (Utils:Property(Properties, "Flag", "Pointer") or (self.Page.Name .. "_" .. self.Name .. "_" .. (Properties.Name or Properties.Text or "Option"))),
									Callback = (Utils:Property(Properties, "Callback", "Function", "Func", "Return")),
									Tooltip = (Utils:Property(Properties, "Tooltip", "Tip") or nil),
								}

								if Content.Tooltip then
									Content.Name = Content.Name .. ' <font color="rgb(180, 180, 180)">(?)</font>'
								end

								Content.Objects["Holder"] = Menu.Objects:Holder(nil, self.Objects["Content"], nil, UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16))
								Content.Objects["Frame"] = Menu.Objects:Outline(nil, Content.Objects["Holder"], UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 4), UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 8), nil, "Light Inline")
								Content.Objects["Text"] = Menu.Objects:Text(true, nil, Content.Objects["Holder"], UDim2.new(0, 37, 0, -1), UDim2.new(1, -37, 1, 0), Content.Name, "Light Text")
								Content.Objects["Button"] = Menu.Objects:Button(Content.Objects["Holder"])
								Menu.Objects:Gradient(Color3.fromRGB(85, 85, 85), -90, nil, Content.Objects["Button"])

								function Content:Set(State)
									Content.State = State
									Menu.Objects:Update(Content.Objects["Frame"], { BackgroundColor3 = (Content.State and "Accent" or "Lighter Inline") })
									Utils:Call(Content.Callback, Content.State)
								end

								function Content:Get(Raw) return Content.State end
								function Content:Picker(Properties) return Menu.Sections.Addons:Picker(Content, Properties) end
								function Content:Bind(Properties) return Menu.Sections.Addons:Bind(Content, Properties) end

								Utils:Connect(Content.Objects["Button"].MouseButton1Down, function()
									Content:Set(not Content.State)
								end)

								Content.Section.Content[Content] = true
								if Content.Flag then Flags[Content.Flag] = Content end
								return Content
							end

							function Menu.Sections:Slider(Properties)
								local Properties = (Properties or {})
								local Content = {
									Objects = {},
									Current = "",
									Tick = tick(),
									Window = self.Window,
									Page = self.Page,
									Section = (self.Section or self),
									State = (Utils:Property(Properties, "State", "Current", "Default", "Def") or 50),
									Maximum = (Utils:Property(Properties, "Maximum", "Max", "Most", "Maximal") or 100),
									Minimum = (Utils:Property(Properties, "Minimum", "Min", "Least", "Minimal") or 0),
									Decimals = (1 / (Utils:Property(Properties, "Decimals", "Rounding", "Tick") or 1)),
									Ending = (Utils:Property(Properties, "Ending", "Suffix", "Sub") or ""),
									Flag = (Utils:Property(Properties, "Flag", "Pointer") or (self.Page.Name .. "_" .. self.Name .. "_" .. (Properties.Name or Properties.Text or "Option"))),
									Callback = (Utils:Property(Properties, "Callback", "Function", "Func", "Return")),
								}

								Content.Objects["Holder"] = Menu.Objects:Holder(nil, self.Objects["Content"], nil, UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18))
								Content.Objects["Outline"] = Menu.Objects:Outline(nil, Content.Objects["Holder"], UDim2.new(0, 36, 0, 5), UDim2.new(1, -69, 0, 8), nil, "Lighter Inline")
								Content.Objects["Frame"] = Menu.Objects:Frame(nil, "Accent", nil, nil, nil, 0, Content.Objects["Outline"], nil, UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0))
								Menu.Objects:Gradient(Color3.fromRGB(85, 85, 85), -90, nil, Content.Objects["Frame"])
								Content.Objects["Value"] = Menu.Objects:Box(nil, Content.Objects["Frame"], UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 3), UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0), "", nil, "Light Text", "Center")
								Content.Objects["Button"] = Menu.Objects:Button(Content.Objects["Holder"])

								function Content:Set(State)
									Content.Last = Content.State
									Content.State = Utils.Math:Normalise(State, Content.Decimals, Content.Minimum, Content.Maximum)
									Content.Current = (Content.State .. Content.Ending)
									Content.Objects["Value"].Text = Content.Current
									Content.Objects["Frame"].Size = UDim2.new((1 - Utils.Math:Percentile(Content.State, Content.Minimum, Content.Maximum)), 0, 1, 0)
									Utils:Call(Content.Callback, Content.State)
								end

								function Content:Get(Raw) return Content.State end

								function Content:Refresh()
									local Location = Utils:Location()
									Content:Set(Utils.Math:Interpolate(Location.X, Content.Objects["Frame"].AbsolutePosition.X, (Content.Objects["Outline"].AbsoluteSize.X - 2), Content.Minimum, Content.Maximum))
								end

								Utils:Connect(Content.Objects["Button"].MouseButton1Down, function()
									if (tick() - Content.Tick) <= 0.2 then
										Content.Tick = 0
										if Content.Last then Content:Set(Content.Last) end
										Content.Objects["Value"]:CaptureFocus()
									else
										Content.Tick = tick()
										Content.Holding = true
										Content.Window.Holds[Content] = "Holding"
										Content:Refresh()
									end
								end)

								Content.Section.Content[Content] = true
								if Content.Flag then Flags[Content.Flag] = Content end
								return Content
							end

							function Menu.Sections:Dropdown(Properties)
								local Properties = (Properties or {})
								local Content = {
									Objects = {},
									Window = self.Window,
									Page = self.Page,
									Section = (self.Section or self),
									State = (Utils:Property(Properties, "State", "Current", "Default", "Def") or 1),
									Shown = (Utils:Property(Properties, "Shown", "Listed", "Viewed") or 5),
									Maximum = (Utils:Property(Properties, "Maximum", "Max", "Most", "Maximal") or 1),
									Minimum = (Utils:Property(Properties, "Minimum", "Min", "Least", "Minimal") or 1),
									Options = (Utils:Property(Properties, "Options", "List") or { "1", "2", "3", "4", "5" }),
									Selected = (Utils:Property(Properties, "Global", "Selected", "Select")),
									Wide = (Utils:Property(Properties, "Wide", "Wider") or false),
									Flag = (Utils:Property(Properties, "Flag", "Pointer") or (self.Page.Name .. "_" .. self.Name .. "_" .. (Properties.Name or Properties.Text or "Option"))),
									Callback = (Utils:Property(Properties, "Callback", "Function", "Func", "Return")),
									NoCallback = (Utils:Property(Properties, "NoCallback", "DontCallback", "IgnoreCallback", "Ignore") or false),
								}

								Content.Objects["Holder"] = Menu.Objects:Holder(nil, self.Objects["Content"], nil, UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 25))
								Content.Objects["Outline"], Content.Objects["Frame"] = Menu.Objects:Outline(nil, Content.Objects["Holder"], UDim2.new(0, (Content.Wide and 24 or 36), 0, 4), UDim2.new(1, (Content.Wide and -49 or -69), 0, 20), "Lighter Background")
								Menu.Objects:Gradient(Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0), -90, 0.6, Content.Objects["Frame"])

								Content.Objects["Arrow"] = Menu.Objects:Create("ImageLabel", {
									BackgroundTransparency = 1,
									BorderSizePixel = 0,
									Parent = Content.Objects["Frame"],
									Position = UDim2.new(1, -13, 0, 7),
									Size = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, 3),
									Image = "rbxassetid://14555080158",
									ImageColor3 = "Accent",
								})
								Content.Objects["Value"] = Menu.Objects:Text(false, nil, Content.Objects["Frame"], UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 0), UDim2.new(1, -22, 1, 0), "", "Light Text", nil, nil, "AtEnd")
								Content.Objects["Button"] = Menu.Objects:Button(Content.Objects["Holder"])

								function Content:Set(State, Loaded)
									if Loaded and typeof(State) == "table" and (#State == 0 or table.find(State, "") ~= nil) then return end
									local ProcessedState
									if typeof(State) == "table" then
										ProcessedState = {}
										for i, value in pairs(State) do
											ProcessedState[i] = (typeof(value) == "number" and Content.Options[value] or tostring(value))
										end
									else
										ProcessedState = (typeof(State) == "number" and Content.Options[State] or tostring(State))
									end
									Content.State = ProcessedState
									local DisplayText = (typeof(ProcessedState) == "table") and table.concat(ProcessedState, ", "):gsub("^,%s*", "") or ProcessedState
									if not DisplayText or DisplayText == "" then DisplayText = "None" end
									Content.Objects["Value"].Text = DisplayText
									Utils:Call(Content.Callback, Content.State)
								end

								function Content:Get(Raw) return Content.State end

								Utils:Connect(Content.Objects["Button"].MouseButton1Down, function()
									Content.Window:Open(Content, "Dropdown")
								end)

								Content.Section.Content[Content] = true
								if not Content.Selected then Content.Selected = Content.Options end
								if Content.Flag then Flags[Content.Flag] = Content end
								return Content
							end

							function Menu.Sections:Divider(Properties)
								local Content = { Objects = {}, Window = self.Window, Page = self.Page, Section = (self.Section or self) }
								Content.Objects["Holder"] = Menu.Objects:Holder(nil, self.Objects["Content"], nil, UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 15))
								Content.Objects["Outline"], Content.Objects["Frame"] = Menu.Objects:Outline(Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Content.Objects["Holder"], UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0), UDim2.new(1, -55, 0, 0), "Lighter Background")
								Menu.Objects:Gradient(Color3.fromRGB(85, 85, 85), -90, nil, Content.Objects["Frame"])
								return Content
							end

							function Menu.Sections.Addons:Holder(Content)
								local Holder = Content.Objects["Addon_Holder"]
								if Holder then
									return Holder
								else
									Content.Objects["Addon_Holder"] = Menu.Objects:Holder(Vector2.new(1, 0), Content.Objects["Holder"], UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, Content.Offset), UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0))
									Menu.Objects:List(nil, Content.Objects["Addon_Holder"], "Horizontal", "Right", "Top")
									return Content.Objects["Addon_Holder"]
								end
							end

							function Menu.Sections.Addons:Picker(Self, Properties)
								local Properties = (Properties or {})
								local Content = {
									Objects = {},
									Window = Self.Window,
									Page = Self.Page,
									Section = Self.Section,
									Self = Self,
									State = (Utils:Property(Properties, "State", "Current", "Default", "Color", "Def") or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)),
									Alpha = (Utils:Property(Properties, "Transparency", "Transp", "Alpha")),
									Flag = (Utils:Property(Properties, "Flag", "Pointer") or (Properties.Name or "Color")),
									Callback = (Utils:Property(Properties, "Callback", "Function", "Func", "Return")),
								}

								Content.Objects["Addon"] = Menu.Sections.Addons:Holder(Self)
								Content.Objects["Holder"] = Menu.Objects:Holder(nil, Content.Objects["Addon"], nil, UDim2.new(0, 20, 1, 0))
								Content.Objects["Outline"] = Menu.Objects:Outline(Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Content.Objects["Holder"], UDim2.new(0, 1, 0.5, 0), UDim2.new(0, 20, 0, 12), nil)
								Content.Objects["Frame"] = Menu.Objects:Frame(nil, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), nil, nil, nil, 0, Content.Objects["Outline"], UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0))
								Menu.Objects:Gradient(Color3.fromRGB(85, 85, 85), -90, nil, Content.Objects["Frame"])
								Content.Objects["Button"] = Menu.Objects:Button(Content.Objects["Holder"])

								function Content:Set(State, Alpha)
									if typeof(State) == "table" then
										Content.State = State
									else
										local Hue, Saturation, Value = State:ToHSV()
										Content.State = { Hue, Saturation, Value, (Content.Alpha and Alpha or nil) }
									end
									local Color = Color3.fromHSV(Content.State[1], Content.State[2], Content.State[3])
									Content.Objects["Frame"].BackgroundColor3 = Color
									Utils:Call(Content.Callback, Color, Content.State[4])
								end

								function Content:Get(Raw)
									if Raw then return Content.State else return Color3.fromHSV(Content.State[1], Content.State[2], Content.State[3]), Content.State[4] end
								end

								Utils:Connect(Content.Objects["Button"].MouseButton1Down, function() Content.Window:Open(Content, "Picker") end)
								Utils:Connect(Content.Objects["Button"].MouseButton2Down, function() Content.Window:Open(Content, "CopyAndPaste") end)

								Content.Section.Content[Content] = true
								if Content.Flag then Flags[(Self.Flag and (Self.Flag .. " ") or "") .. Content.Flag] = Content end
								return Content
							end

							function Menu.Sections.Addons:Bind(Self, Properties)
								local Properties = (Properties or {})
								local Content = {
									Objects = {},
									Active = false,
									Window = Self.Window,
									Page = Self.Page,
									Section = Self.Section,
									Self = Self,
									Name = (Utils:Property(Properties, "Name", "Text")),
									State = (Utils:Property(Properties, "State", "Current", "Default", "Bind", "Def")),
									Mode = (Utils:Property(Properties, "Mode", "Type") or "On Hold"),
									Disable = (Utils:Property(Properties, "Disabled", "Disable", "NoMode") or false),
									Flag = (Utils:Property(Properties, "Flag", "Pointer") or (Properties.Name or "Bind")),
									Callback = (Utils:Property(Properties, "Callback", "Function", "Func", "Return")),
								}

								Content.Objects["Addon"] = Menu.Sections.Addons:Holder(Self)
								Content.Objects["Holder"] = Menu.Objects:Holder(nil, Content.Objects["Addon"], nil, UDim2.new(0, 20, 1, 0))
								Content.Objects["Value"] = Menu.Objects:Text(false, Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Content.Objects["Holder"], UDim2.new(0, 1, 0.5, 0), UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 9), "", "Dark Text", nil, nil, nil, 8)
								Content.Objects["Button"] = Menu.Objects:Button(Content.Objects["Holder"])

								function Content:Set(State)
									if typeof(State) == "table" then
										Content.Mode = (State[3] or Content.Mode)
										Content.State = State
									else
										Content.State = ((State and (State.EnumType or State.KeyCode)) and { State.EnumType == Enum.KeyCode and "KeyCode" or "UserInputType", State.Name } or {})
									end
									Content:Adjust(Content.Mode)
									Content.Short = (#Content.State > 0 and (Menu.Inputs.Shortened[Content.State[2]] or Content.State[2]))
									Content.Objects["Value"].Text = string.format("[%s]", Content.Short or "...")
									if Content.Item then Content.Item:Update() end
								end

								function Content:Get(Raw)
									if Raw then
										if #Content.State > 0 then return Content.State[1][Content.State[2]] end
										return Content.State
									else
										return Content.State
									end
								end

								function Content:Adjust(Mode)
									Content.Mode = Mode
									Content.Active = (Content.Mode == "Off Hold" or Content.Mode == "Off Toggle" or Content.Mode == "Always")
									if Content.Item then Content.Item:Update() end
								end

								function Content:Update(Type)
									local Previous = Content.Active
									if Content.Mode ~= "Always" then
										if Content.Mode == "On Hold" or Content.Mode == "Off Hold" then
											Content.Active = not Content.Active
										elseif Content.Mode == "On Toggle" or Content.Mode == "Off Toggle" then
											if Type then Content.Active = not Content.Active end
										end
									end
									if Content.Active ~= Previous then
										Utils:Call(Content.Callback, Content.Active, Previous)
										if Content.Item then Content.Item:Update() end
									end
								end

								Utils:Connect(Content.Objects["Button"].MouseButton1Down, function() Content.Window:Open(Content, "Bind") end)
								Utils:Connect(Content.Objects["Button"].MouseButton2Down, function()
									if not Content.Disable then Content.Window:Open(Content, "BindMode") end
								end)

								Content.Section.Content[Content] = true
								Content.Window.Binds[Content] = true
								if Content.Flag then Flags[(Self.Flag and (Self.Flag .. " ") or "") .. Content.Flag] = Content end
								return Content
							end
						end
					end
				end)()
			end
		end

		return Utils, Menu, Flags, Character, Visuals2, Env, Window
	end)()
end
--[[ END INLINED NEW UI LIBRARY ]]

--[[ BEGIN PIN.RETA NEW UI COMPATIBILITY LAYER ]]
-- pin.reta Linoria-to-newui compatibility adapter
-- Keeps the original pin.reta control declarations and state access API intact.
local function CreatePinRetaNewUIAdapter(NewMenu, NewUtils, NewFlags)
    local Environment = getgenv()
    Environment.Toggles = Environment.Toggles or {}
    Environment.Options = Environment.Options or {}

    local function safeCall(Callback, ...)
        if type(Callback) ~= "function" then
            return true
        end

        return pcall(Callback, ...)
    end

    local function ensureFolder(Path)
        if not isfolder(Path) then
            makefolder(Path)
        end
    end

    -- The supplied library references this method from SettingsPage but does not
    -- define it.  Keeping theme-linked instances in sync also makes presets and
    -- color-picker edits immediately visible.
    function NewMenu.Presets:Update(Theme, Color)
        NewMenu.Themes[Theme] = Color

        for Object, Properties in next, NewMenu.Storage do
            for Property, StoredTheme in next, Properties do
                if StoredTheme == Theme then
                    pcall(function()
                        Object[Property] = Color
                    end)
                end
            end
        end
    end

    ensureFolder(NewMenu.FileName)
    ensureFolder(NewMenu.FileName .. "/Configs")

    -- Fix numeric color decoding and preserve colons inside string values.  The
    -- source implementation feeds string components directly to Color3.fromHSV
    -- and splits every colon, which can make otherwise valid configs fail.
    function NewUtils.Configs:Set(Config)
        for Line in (tostring(Config or "") .. "\n"):gmatch("([^\n]*)\n") do
            local FlagName, Encoded = Line:match("^([^:]+):%s*(.*)$")
            local Flag = FlagName and NewFlags[FlagName]
            if not Flag and FlagName then
                local Suffix = " " .. FlagName
                for StoredName, StoredFlag in pairs(NewFlags) do
                    if type(StoredName) == "string" and StoredName:sub(-#Suffix) == Suffix then
                        Flag = StoredFlag
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
            if Flag and Encoded then
                local Value = Encoded
                local Alpha
                local Inner = Encoded:match("^rgb%((.*)%)$")

                if Inner then
                    local Parts = string.split(Inner, ",")
                    Value = Color3.fromHSV(
                        tonumber(Parts[1]) or 0,
                        tonumber(Parts[2]) or 0,
                        tonumber(Parts[3]) or 0
                    )
                    Alpha = tonumber(Parts[4])
                else
                    Inner = Encoded:match("^key%((.*)%)$")
                    if Inner then
                        local Parts = string.split(Inner, ",")
                        Value = {
                            Parts[1] ~= "nil" and Parts[1] or nil,
                            Parts[2] ~= "nil" and Parts[2] or nil,
                            Parts[3] ~= "nil" and Parts[3] or nil,
                        }
                    else
                        Inner = Encoded:match("^bool%((.*)%)$")
                        if Inner then
                            Value = Inner == "true"
                        else
                            Inner = Encoded:match("^table%((.*)%)$")
                            if Inner then
                                Value = Inner == "" and {} or string.split(Inner, ",")
                            else
                                Inner = Encoded:match("^string%((.*)%)$")
                                if Inner ~= nil then
                                    Value = Inner
                                else
                                    Inner = Encoded:match("^number%((.*)%)$")
                                    if Inner then
                                        Value = tonumber(Inner)
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end

                if Alpha ~= nil then
                    Flag:Set(Value, Alpha)
                else
                    Flag:Set(Value)
                end
            end
        end
    end

    local Library = {
        Opened = false,
        ToggleKeybind = Enum.KeyCode.RightShift,
        Scale = 1.25,
        _windows = {},
        _keybinds = {},
        _initialised = false,
    }

    function Library:_scaleObject(Object)
        if not Object then
            return nil
        end

        local Existing = Object:FindFirstChild("PinRetaUIScale")
        if Existing and Existing:IsA("UIScale") then
            Existing.Scale = self.Scale
            return Existing
        end

        local Scale = Instance.new("UIScale")
        Scale.Name = "PinRetaUIScale"
        Scale.Scale = self.Scale
        Scale.Parent = Object
        return Scale
    end

    -- Drawing-based HUD panels cannot be registered in Menu.Storage like GUI
    -- instances, so expose the live palette for them to sample every frame.
    function Library:GetThemeColor(Name, Fallback)
        local Color = NewMenu.Themes[Name]
        return typeof(Color) == "Color3" and Color or Fallback
    end

    local Control = {}
    Control.__index = Control

    function Control:_emit(...)
        for _, Callback in ipairs(self._callbacks) do
            safeCall(Callback, ...)
        end
    end

    function Control:OnChanged(Callback)
        if type(Callback) == "function" then
            self._callbacks[#self._callbacks + 1] = Callback
        end
        return self
    end

    local function keyNameFromState(State)
        if typeof(State) == "EnumItem" then
            return State.Name
        end
        if type(State) == "table" then
            return State[2] or "None"
        end
        if type(State) == "string" and State ~= "" then
            return State
        end
        return "None"
    end

    local function enumFromKey(State)
        if typeof(State) == "EnumItem" or type(State) == "table" then
            return State
        end
        if type(State) ~= "string" or State == "" or State == "None" then
            return {}
        end

        return Enum.KeyCode[State] or Enum.UserInputType[State] or {}
    end

    local function toNewBindMode(Mode)
        local Normalised = string.lower(tostring(Mode or "Toggle"))
        if Normalised == "hold" or Normalised == "on hold" then
            return "On Hold"
        elseif Normalised == "off hold" then
            return "Off Hold"
        elseif Normalised == "off toggle" then
            return "Off Toggle"
        elseif Normalised == "always" then
            return "Always"
        end
        return "On Toggle"
    end

    local function fromNewBindMode(Mode)
        if Mode == "On Hold" or Mode == "Off Hold" then
            return "Hold"
        elseif Mode == "Always" then
            return "Always"
        end
        return "Toggle"
    end

    function Control:SetValue(Value)
        if self._kind == "keybind" then
            self._raw:Set(enumFromKey(Value))
        elseif self._raw and self._raw.Set then
            self._raw:Set(Value)
        else
            self.Value = Value
            self:_emit(Value)
        end
        return self
    end

    function Control:SetValueRGB(Value, Transparency)
        if self._kind == "color" and self._raw then
            self._raw:Set(Value, Transparency)
        else
            self:SetValue(Value)
        end
        return self
    end

    function Control:SetValueHSV(Hue, Saturation, Brightness, Transparency)
        return self:SetValueRGB(Color3.fromHSV(Hue, Saturation, Brightness), Transparency)
    end

    function Control:GetState()
        if self._kind == "keybind" then
            return self.State == true
        end
        return self.Value
    end

    function Control:SetText(Text)
        self.Text = tostring(Text)
        if self._raw then
            self._raw.Name = self.Text
            local TextObject = self._raw.Objects and self._raw.Objects["Text"]
            if TextObject then
                TextObject.Text = self.Text
            end
        end
        return self
    end

    function Control:SetValues(Values)
        if self._kind ~= "dropdown" or not self._raw then
            return self
        end

        self.Values = Values or {}
        self._raw.Options = self.Values
        self._raw.Selected = self.Values
        self._raw.Maximum = self.Multi and math.max(#self.Values, 1) or 1

        if self.Multi then
            local Filtered = {}
            for _, Value in ipairs(type(self.Value) == "table" and self.Value or {}) do
                if table.find(self.Values, Value) then
                    Filtered[#Filtered + 1] = Value
                end
            end
            self:SetValue(Filtered)
        else
            local NextValue = self.Value
            if not table.find(self.Values, NextValue) then
                NextValue = self.Values[1] or "None"
            end
            self:SetValue(NextValue)
        end

        return self
    end

    function Control:AddColorPicker(Id, Properties)
        return self._library:_addColorPicker(self._addonHost or self._raw, Id, Properties)
    end

    function Control:AddKeyPicker(Id, Properties)
        return self._library:_addKeyPicker(self._addonHost or self._raw, self, Id, Properties)
    end

    local function makeControl(Kind, Value, LibraryRef)
        return setmetatable({
            Value = Value,
            _kind = Kind,
            _library = LibraryRef,
            _callbacks = {},
        }, Control)
    end

    local Container = {}
    Container.__index = Container

    function Container:AddToggle(Id, Properties)
        local Props = Properties or {}
        local Initial = Props.Default == true
        local Wrapper = makeControl("toggle", Initial, self._library)
        if type(Props.Callback) == "function" then
            Wrapper._callbacks[#Wrapper._callbacks + 1] = Props.Callback
        end

        local Raw = self._raw:Toggle({
            Name = Props.Text or Id,
            State = Initial,
            Flag = Id,
            Tooltip = Props.Tooltip,
            Callback = function(Value)
                Wrapper.Value = Value == true
                Wrapper:_emit(Wrapper.Value)
            end,
        })

        Wrapper._raw = Raw
        Wrapper._addonHost = Raw
        Wrapper.Text = Props.Text or Id
        Environment.Toggles[Id] = Wrapper
        return Wrapper
    end

    function Container:AddSlider(Id, Properties)
        local Props = Properties or {}
        local Initial = tonumber(Props.Default) or tonumber(Props.Min) or 0
        local Digits = tonumber(Props.Rounding) or 0
        local Wrapper = makeControl("slider", Initial, self._library)
        if type(Props.Callback) == "function" then
            Wrapper._callbacks[#Wrapper._callbacks + 1] = Props.Callback
        end

        local Raw = self._raw:Slider({
            Name = Props.Text or Id,
            State = Initial,
            Minimum = tonumber(Props.Min) or 0,
            Maximum = tonumber(Props.Max) or 100,
            Decimals = 10 ^ -Digits,
            Ending = Props.Suffix or "",
            Flag = Id,
            Callback = function(Value)
                Wrapper.Value = Value
                Wrapper:_emit(Value)
            end,
        })

        Wrapper._raw = Raw
        Wrapper.Text = Props.Text or Id
        Environment.Options[Id] = Wrapper

        if Raw.Objects and Raw.Objects["Value"] then
            NewUtils:Connect(Raw.Objects["Value"].FocusLost, function()
                local Number = tonumber(Raw.Objects["Value"].Text)
                if Number then
                    Raw:Set(Number)
                else
                    Raw.Objects["Value"].Text = tostring(Raw.State) .. tostring(Raw.Ending or "")
                end
            end)
        end

        return Wrapper
    end

    local function normaliseDropdownDefault(Values, Default, Multi)
        if Multi then
            if type(Default) ~= "table" then
                return {}
            end

            local Result = {}
            for Index, Value in pairs(Default) do
                if type(Index) == "number" then
                    Result[#Result + 1] = type(Value) == "number" and Values[Value] or Value
                elseif Value == true then
                    Result[#Result + 1] = Index
                end
            end
            return Result
        end

        if type(Default) == "number" then
            return Values[Default] or Values[1] or "None"
        end
        return Default or Values[1] or "None"
    end

    function Container:AddDropdown(Id, Properties)
        local Props = Properties or {}
        local Values = Props.Values or {}
        local Multi = Props.Multi == true
        local Initial = normaliseDropdownDefault(Values, Props.Default, Multi)
        local Wrapper = makeControl("dropdown", Initial, self._library)
        Wrapper.Values = Values
        Wrapper.Multi = Multi
        if type(Props.Callback) == "function" then
            Wrapper._callbacks[#Wrapper._callbacks + 1] = Props.Callback
        end

        local Raw = self._raw:Dropdown({
            Name = Props.Text or Id,
            State = Initial,
            Options = Values,
            Selected = Values,
            Maximum = Multi and math.max(#Values, 1) or 1,
            Minimum = Multi and 0 or 1,
            Shown = tonumber(Props.Shown) or 8,
            Wide = Props.Wide,
            Flag = Id,
            Callback = function(Value)
                Wrapper.Value = Value
                Wrapper:_emit(Value)
            end,
        })

        Wrapper._raw = Raw
        Wrapper.Text = Props.Text or Id
        Environment.Options[Id] = Wrapper
        return Wrapper
    end

    function Container:AddInput(Id, Properties)
        local Props = Properties or {}
        local Initial = tostring(Props.Default or "")
        local Wrapper = makeControl("input", Initial, self._library)
        if type(Props.Callback) == "function" then
            Wrapper._callbacks[#Wrapper._callbacks + 1] = Props.Callback
        end

        local Raw = self._raw:Box({
            Name = Props.Text or Id,
            State = Initial,
            Holder = Props.Placeholder or Props.Text or Id,
            Flag = Id,
            Callback = function(Value)
                Wrapper.Value = tostring(Value or "")
                Wrapper:_emit(Wrapper.Value)
            end,
        })

        Wrapper._raw = Raw
        Wrapper.Text = Props.Text or Id
        Environment.Options[Id] = Wrapper

        if Raw.Objects and Raw.Objects["Button"] and Raw.Objects["Value"] then
            Raw.Objects["Value"].ClearTextOnFocus = Props.ClearText == true
            NewUtils:Connect(Raw.Objects["Button"].MouseButton1Down, function()
                Raw.Objects["Value"]:CaptureFocus()
            end)
            NewUtils:Connect(Raw.Objects["Value"].FocusLost, function()
                Raw:Set(Raw.Objects["Value"].Text)
            end)
        end

        return Wrapper
    end

    function Container:AddButton(Text, Callback)
        local Name = Text
        local Function = Callback
        if type(Text) == "table" then
            Name = Text.Text or Text.Name or "button"
            Function = Text.Func or Text.Callback
        end

        local Raw = self._raw:Button({ Name = tostring(Name), Callback = Function })
        local Wrapper = makeControl("button", nil, self._library)
        Wrapper._raw = Raw
        Wrapper.Text = tostring(Name)
        return Wrapper
    end

    function Container:AddLabel(Text)
        local Raw = self._raw:Label({ Name = tostring(Text) })
        local Wrapper = makeControl("label", tostring(Text), self._library)
        Wrapper._raw = Raw
        Wrapper._addonHost = Raw
        Wrapper.Text = tostring(Text)
        return Wrapper
    end

    function Container:AddDependencyBox()
        local Raw = self._raw:Holder({ Callback = function() end })
        local Wrapper = setmetatable({
            _raw = Raw,
            _library = self._library,
            Frame = Raw.Objects and Raw.Objects["Holder"],
            _dependencies = {},
        }, Container)
        return Wrapper
    end

    function Container:SetupDependencies(Dependencies)
        self._dependencies = Dependencies or {}

        local function updateVisibility()
            local Visible = true
            for _, Dependency in ipairs(self._dependencies) do
                local Option = Dependency[1]
                local Expected = Dependency[2]
                if not Option or Option.Value ~= Expected then
                    Visible = false
                    break
                end
            end
            self._raw:Set(Visible)
        end

        for _, Dependency in ipairs(self._dependencies) do
            local Option = Dependency[1]
            if Option and Option.OnChanged then
                Option:OnChanged(updateVisibility)
            end
        end

        updateVisibility()
        return self
    end

    local TabBox = {}
    TabBox.__index = TabBox

    function TabBox:AddTab(Name)
        local RawSection = self._page._raw:Section({ Name = Name, Side = self._side })
        return setmetatable({
            _raw = RawSection,
            _library = self._library,
            Frame = RawSection.Objects and RawSection.Objects["Frame"],
        }, Container)
    end

    local Page = {}
    Page.__index = Page

    local function makeSection(PageWrapper, Name, Side)
        local RawSection = PageWrapper._raw:Section({ Name = Name, Side = Side })
        return setmetatable({
            _raw = RawSection,
            _library = PageWrapper._library,
            Frame = RawSection.Objects and RawSection.Objects["Frame"],
        }, Container)
    end

    function Page:AddLeftGroupbox(Name)
        return makeSection(self, Name, "Left")
    end

    function Page:AddRightGroupbox(Name)
        return makeSection(self, Name, "Right")
    end

    function Page:AddLeftTabbox()
        return setmetatable({ _page = self, _side = "Left", _library = self._library }, TabBox)
    end

    function Page:AddRightTabbox()
        return setmetatable({ _page = self, _side = "Right", _library = self._library }, TabBox)
    end

    local Window = {}
    Window.__index = Window

    function Window:AddTab(Name)
        local RawPage = self._raw:Page({ Name = Name })
        local Wrapper = setmetatable({ _raw = RawPage, _library = self._library }, Page)
        self._pages[#self._pages + 1] = Wrapper
        return Wrapper
    end

    function Library:_addColorPicker(Host, Id, Properties)
        local Props = Properties or {}
        local Initial = Props.Default or Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
        local Wrapper = makeControl("color", Initial, self)
        Wrapper.Transparency = Props.Transparency
        if type(Props.Callback) == "function" then
            Wrapper._callbacks[#Wrapper._callbacks + 1] = Props.Callback
        end

        local Raw = Host:Picker({
            Name = Props.Title or Props.Text or Id,
            State = Initial,
            Transparency = Props.Transparency,
            Flag = Id,
            Callback = function(Color, Transparency)
                Wrapper.Value = Color
                Wrapper.Transparency = Transparency
                Wrapper:_emit(Color, Transparency)
            end,
        })

        Wrapper._raw = Raw
        Wrapper._addonHost = Host
        Wrapper.Text = Props.Title or Props.Text or Id
        Environment.Options[Id] = Wrapper
        return Wrapper
    end

    function Library:_addKeyPicker(Host, ParentControl, Id, Properties)
        local Props = Properties or {}
        local InitialName = keyNameFromState(Props.Default)
        local Wrapper = makeControl("keybind", InitialName, self)
        Wrapper.Mode = Props.Mode or "Toggle"
        Wrapper.SyncToggleState = Props.SyncToggleState == true
        if type(Props.Callback) == "function" then
            Wrapper._callbacks[#Wrapper._callbacks + 1] = Props.Callback
        end

        local Raw = Host:Bind({
            Name = Props.Text or Id,
            State = enumFromKey(Props.Default),
            Mode = toNewBindMode(Props.Mode),
            Flag = Id,
            Disabled = Props.NoModes == true,
            Callback = function(Active)
                Wrapper.Active = Active
                Wrapper.State = Active
                if Wrapper.SyncToggleState and ParentControl and ParentControl._kind == "toggle" then
                    ParentControl:SetValue(Active)
                end
                Wrapper:_emit(Active)
            end,
        })

        Wrapper._raw = Raw
        Wrapper._addonHost = Host
        Wrapper.Text = Props.Text or Id
        Wrapper.State = Raw.Active

        local OriginalSet = Raw.Set
        Raw.Set = function(Self, State)
            OriginalSet(Self, enumFromKey(State))
            Wrapper.Value = keyNameFromState(Self.State)
            Wrapper.Mode = fromNewBindMode(Self.Mode)
            Wrapper.State = Self.Active
        end

        local OriginalAdjust = Raw.Adjust
        Raw.Adjust = function(Self, Mode)
            OriginalAdjust(Self, Mode)
            Wrapper.Mode = fromNewBindMode(Self.Mode)
            Wrapper.State = Self.Active
        end

        -- Use the adapter's deterministic dispatcher below.  Leaving this entry
        -- in the source library's Window.Binds table would process every input
        -- twice and make toggle-mode binds appear to do nothing.
        Raw.Window.Binds[Raw] = nil
        self._keybinds[Raw] = Wrapper

        if self._bindIndicator and Props.NoUI ~= true then
            Raw.Item = self._bindIndicator:Insert(Raw)
        end

        Environment.Options[Id] = Wrapper
        return Wrapper
    end

    function Library:_updateKeybinds(Input, Began)
        if not self._initialised then
            return
        end

        local InputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
        if InputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then
            return
        end

        local RawWindow = self._window and self._window._raw
        if RawWindow and next(RawWindow.Opened.Bind) ~= nil then
            return
        end

        for Raw in pairs(self._keybinds) do
            local State = Raw.State
            if type(State) == "table" and State[1] and State[2] then
                local EnumType = Enum[State[1]]
                local BoundInput = EnumType and EnumType[State[2]]
                if BoundInput and (Input.KeyCode == BoundInput or Input.UserInputType == BoundInput) then
                    Raw:Update(Began)
                end
            end
        end
    end

    function Library:CreateWindow(Properties)
        local Props = Properties or {}
        local Raw = NewMenu:Create({
            Name = Props.Title or Props.Name or "pin.reta V2",
            Stamp = "new ui",
            Size = Props.Size or Vector2.new(620, 500),
            Scrollable = true,
        })

        local Wrapper = setmetatable({
            _raw = Raw,
            _library = self,
            _pages = {},
        }, Window)

        self._windows[#self._windows + 1] = Wrapper
        self._window = Wrapper
        self._bindIndicator = Raw:Indicators()
        self:_scaleObject(self._bindIndicator.Objects["Outline"])

        self.KeybindFrame = setmetatable({}, {
            __index = function(_, Key)
                if Key == "Visible" then
                    return self._bindIndicator.State
                end
            end,
            __newindex = function(_, Key, Value)
                if Key == "Visible" then
                    self._bindIndicator:Set(Value == true)
                end
            end,
        })

        local OriginalSwitch = Raw.Switch
        Raw.Switch = function(Self, State)
            OriginalSwitch(Self, State)
            Library.Opened = Self.State
        end

        Raw:Switch(Props.AutoShow == true)

        local InputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
        NewUtils:Connect(InputService.InputBegan, function(Input, Processed)
            if not Processed and Input.KeyCode == Library.ToggleKeybind then
                Raw:Switch(not Raw.State)
            end
            Library:_updateKeybinds(Input, true)
        end)

        NewUtils:Connect(InputService.InputEnded, function(Input)
            Library:_updateKeybinds(Input, false)
        end)

        return Wrapper
    end

    function Library:Notify(Title, Message, Duration, BgColor)
        local Text
        if Message ~= nil then
            Text = tostring(Title) .. ": " .. tostring(Message)
        else
            Text = tostring(Title)
        end

        local Toast = NewMenu:Toast({ Text = Text, Duration = tonumber(Duration) or 4, BgColor = BgColor })
        self:_scaleObject(Toast.Objects["Outline"])
        task.delay(tonumber(Duration) or 4, function()
            if Toast and Toast.State then
                Toast:Remove()
            end
        end)
        return Toast
    end

    function Library:_initialise()
        if self._initialised or not self._window then
            return
        end

        local RawWindow = self._window._raw
        RawWindow:Initialise()
        if self._window._pages[1] then
            self._window._pages[1]._raw:Set(true)
        end
        self:_scaleObject(RawWindow.Objects["Screen_Outline"])
        RawWindow.Objects["Screen_Outline"].Position = UDim2.new(
            0.5,
            -(RawWindow.Size.X * self.Scale) / 2,
            0.5,
            -(RawWindow.Size.Y * self.Scale) / 2
        )
        self._initialised = true
    end

    local SaveManager = {
        Folder = "Pin.Reta",
        Library = Library,
    }

    function SaveManager:SetOptionsTEMP()
        return self
    end

    function SaveManager:SetLibrary(Value)
        self.Library = Value or Library
        return self
    end

    function SaveManager:IgnoreThemeSettings()
        return self
    end

    function SaveManager:SetFolder(Folder)
        self.Folder = Folder or self.Folder
        NewMenu.FileName = self.Folder
        ensureFolder(self.Folder)
        ensureFolder(self.Folder .. "/Configs")
        return self
    end

    function SaveManager:_configPath(Name)
        local Sanitised = tostring(Name or "")
            :gsub("%.cfg$", "")
            :gsub("[^%w_%- ]", "")
            :sub(1, 64)
        if Sanitised == "" or Sanitised == "None" then
            return nil, nil
        end
        return self.Folder .. "/Configs/" .. Sanitised .. ".cfg", Sanitised
    end

    function SaveManager:_configNames()
        local Names = {}
        local Success, Files = pcall(listfiles, self.Folder .. "/Configs")
        if Success then
            for _, File in ipairs(Files) do
                local Normalised = tostring(File):gsub("\\", "/")
                local Name = Normalised:match("([^/]+)%.cfg$")
                if Name then
                    Names[#Names + 1] = Name
                end
            end
        end
        table.sort(Names)
        if #Names == 0 then
            Names[1] = "None"
        end
        return Names
    end

    function SaveManager:_selectedName()
        if not self.Controls then
            return nil
        end
        local Typed = self.Controls.Name.Value
        if Typed and Typed ~= "" then
            return Typed
        end
        return self.Controls.List.Value
    end

    function SaveManager:RefreshConfigList()
        if self.Controls then
            self.Controls.List:SetValues(self:_configNames())
        end
    end

    function SaveManager:Save(Name)
        local Path, CleanName = self:_configPath(Name or self:_selectedName())
        if not Path then
            self.Library:Notify("Config", "Enter a valid config name")
            return false
        end

        local Success, Error = pcall(writefile, Path, NewUtils.Configs:Get())
        if Success then
            self:RefreshConfigList()
            self.Controls.List:SetValue(CleanName)
            self.Library:Notify("Config", "Saved " .. CleanName)
        else
            self.Library:Notify("Config", "Save failed: " .. tostring(Error))
        end
        return Success
    end

    function SaveManager:Load(Name, Silent)
        local Path, CleanName = self:_configPath(Name or self:_selectedName())
        if not Path or not isfile(Path) then
            if not Silent then
                self.Library:Notify("Config", "Config not found")
            end
            return false
        end

        local Success, Error = pcall(function()
            NewUtils.Configs:Set(readfile(Path))
        end)
        if not Silent then
            self.Library:Notify("Config", Success and ("Loaded " .. CleanName) or ("Load failed: " .. tostring(Error)))
        end
        return Success
    end

    function SaveManager:Delete(Name)
        local Path, CleanName = self:_configPath(Name or self:_selectedName())
        if not Path or not isfile(Path) then
            self.Library:Notify("Config", "Config not found")
            return false
        end

        local Success, Error = pcall(delfile, Path)
        if Success then
            self:RefreshConfigList()
            self.Library:Notify("Config", "Deleted " .. CleanName)
        else
            self.Library:Notify("Config", "Delete failed: " .. tostring(Error))
        end
        return Success
    end

    function SaveManager:SetAutoload(Name)
        local Path, CleanName = self:_configPath(Name or self:_selectedName())
        if not Path or not isfile(Path) then
            self.Library:Notify("Config", "Save the config before setting autoload")
            return false
        end

        local Success, Error = pcall(writefile, self.Folder .. "/autoload.txt", CleanName)
        self.Library:Notify("Config", Success and ("Autoload set to " .. CleanName) or ("Autoload failed: " .. tostring(Error)))
        return Success
    end

    function SaveManager:BuildConfigSection(Tab)
        if self.Controls then
            return
        end

        local Section = Tab:AddRightGroupbox("CONFIGS")
        local Names = self:_configNames()
        self.Controls = {
            List = Section:AddDropdown("Preset Configurations", {
                Text = "configs",
                Values = Names,
                Default = 1,
            }),
            Name = Section:AddInput("Preset Name", {
                Text = "config name",
                Default = "",
            }),
        }

        Section:AddButton("save config", function()
            self:Save()
        end)
        Section:AddButton("load config", function()
            self:Load()
        end)
        Section:AddButton("delete config", function()
            self:Delete()
        end)
        Section:AddButton("refresh configs", function()
            self:RefreshConfigList()
        end)
        Section:AddButton("set autoload", function()
            self:SetAutoload()
        end)
    end

    function SaveManager:LoadAutoloadConfig()
        self.Library:_initialise()

        local AutoloadPath = self.Folder .. "/autoload.txt"
        if isfile(AutoloadPath) then
            local Success, Name = pcall(readfile, AutoloadPath)
            if Success and Name and Name ~= "" then
                self:Load(Name, true)
            end
        end
    end

    local ThemeManager = {
        Folder = "Pin.Reta",
        Library = Library,
    }

    function ThemeManager:SetOptionsTEMP()
        return self
    end

    function ThemeManager:SetLibrary(Value)
        self.Library = Value or Library
        return self
    end

    function ThemeManager:SetFolder(Folder)
        self.Folder = Folder or self.Folder
        return self
    end

    function ThemeManager:ApplyToGroupbox(Groupbox)
        local PresetNames = {}
        for Name in pairs(NewMenu.Presets.Themes) do
            PresetNames[#PresetNames + 1] = Name
        end
        table.sort(PresetNames)

        local ThemeControls = {}
        Groupbox:AddDropdown("__newui_theme_preset", {
            Text = "theme preset",
            Values = PresetNames,
            Default = table.find(PresetNames, "pulse") or 1,
            Callback = function(Preset)
                local Theme = NewMenu.Presets.Themes[Preset]
                if Theme then
                    for Name, Color in pairs(Theme) do
                        NewMenu.Presets:Update(Name, Color)
                        if ThemeControls[Name] then
                            ThemeControls[Name]:SetValue(Color)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end,
        })

        local ThemeOrder = {
            "Accent",
            "Arrow Color",
            "Lighter Background",
            "Light Background",
            "Dark Background",
            "Outline",
            "Lighter Inline",
            "Light Inline",
            "Dark Inline",
            "Light Text",
            "Dark Text",
        }

        for _, Name in ipairs(ThemeOrder) do
            if NewMenu.Themes[Name] then
                local Label = Groupbox:AddLabel(Name)
                local Id = "__newui_theme_" .. Name:gsub("%W", "_")
                ThemeControls[Name] = Label:AddColorPicker(Id, {
                    Title = Name,
                    Default = NewMenu.Themes[Name],
                    Callback = function(Color)
                        NewMenu.Presets:Update(Name, Color)
                    end,
                })
            end
        end
    end

    Library.SaveManager = SaveManager
    Library.ThemeManager = ThemeManager
    return Library
end

-- The source UI's Tabbox adapter creates one standalone section per AddTab.
-- These routers intentionally return a shared section, inserting a subheading
-- before each feature group so related controls live inside one window.
local function make_merged_tabbox(Section)
    local Count = 0
    return {
        AddTab = function(_, Name)
            Count += 1
            if Count > 1 then
                Section:AddLabel(" ")
            end
            Section:AddLabel("--- " .. string.upper(tostring(Name)) .. " ---")
            return Section
        end,
    }
end

local esp_inventory_section = ui.tabs.visuals:AddLeftGroupbox('ESP / INVENTORY')
local esp_inventory_box = make_merged_tabbox(esp_inventory_section)

local visual_tools_section = ui.tabs.visuals:AddRightGroupbox('VISUAL TOOLS')
local visual_tools_box = make_merged_tabbox(visual_tools_section)

local character_fly_section = ui.tabs.misc:AddLeftGroupbox('CHARACTER / FLY HACK')
local desync_section = ui.tabs.misc:AddLeftGroupbox('DESYNC')
local move_section_counts = {}
local move_box = {
    AddTab = function(_, Name)
        local Normalised = string.upper(tostring(Name))
        local Section = Normalised == 'DESYNC' and desync_section or character_fly_section
        local Count = move_section_counts[Section] or 0
        if Count > 0 then
            Section:AddLabel(" ")
        end
        Section:AddLabel("--- " .. Normalised .. " ---")
        move_section_counts[Section] = Count + 1
        return Section
    end,
}

local misc_movement_box = make_merged_tabbox(misc_movement_section)

    local function get_item_icon(name)

        local items_list = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("ItemsList")

            local item_folder = items_list:FindFirstChild(name)

                end
            end

        item_icon_cache[name] = false
        return false
